# The people named in PostgreSQL commits and mail.  See README.md.
#
# A stanza per person, separated by blank lines:
#
#   Canonical: Name <address>   how to show them; the address is optional
#   Alternate: Name <address>   another name and address they're known by,
#                               or a name or address on its own
#   Kind: none                  not a person: a tool, a form, a stray word
#   Distinct: Name <address>    someone else, who isn't this person
#
# Addresses are written as in data/mail, or plainly.  update-people.py only
# adds to this file; merge, split and rename by editing it.
